West of House
You are standing in an open field west of a white house, with a boarded front door.
There is a small mailbox here.


Starting location: West House
Target score: 80
Model: openai/gpt-4o-mini | Agent type: smart
Maximum steps: 500

LESSONS LEARNED FROM PREVIOUS EPISODES:
Repeat these positive behaviors:
1. From Behind House, open the window and go west; 'west' into the Kitchen recorded +10. Repeat this opening immediately, without detours.
2. In the Living Room, push the rug, open the trap door, then 'down'; the +25 was recorded on 'down'. Do this early.
3. Before descending, check inventory and confirm the lantern is carried and lit; the Cellar was still reported pitch black despite 'lantern is now on'.
4. On entering a room, read the text, list the exits, and try each untried exit or verb (look, up, examine) once.
5. Take visible valuables in new rooms, like the Gallery art (+4, prior episode), and keep them carried until banked.
Avoid these negative behaviors:
1. Never take a movement command in a pitch-black room; 'south' from the dark Cellar led to a grue death (-10, game over). Check light or 'look' first.
2. Do not shuttle West House/North House or put and re-take the leaflet; about 8 opening steps produced no score.
3. Drop the stale Maze 'take coins' goal when the route is unknown; it steered the early actions off-plan.
4. Avoid prior-episode fatal guesses: 'jump' at East Chasm, 'give troll painting' (-10, ended the game), and entering the Troll room.
5. Do not cycle Cellar, East Chasm, Gallery and Studio (about 150 steps, no score, prior episode). After two repeats, change verb; try Studio 'look' and 'up'.
Apply these lessons when relevant; current game feedback takes precedence.

Historian episode review — episode 39
================================================

Review source: llm
Lessons: 5 positive + 5 negative (configured per category: 5)

Confirmed score events: +10 on 'west' into the Kitchen, +25 on 'down' to the Cellar, +4 on 'take art', and -10 from a grue death, ending at 29. The early window and trap-door progress and the Gallery art were the strongest decisions. After that, about 350 steps earned nothing. The Actor cycled Cellar, East Chasm, Gallery and Studio, repeatedly toggled the sack and lantern (put/take), and took garlic and lunch without payoff. All transitions were confirmed, so symbolic navigation worked, but the planner re-issued the stale Maze 'take coins' goal about 20 times and the Actor never discarded it. The worst failure was entering the Troll room in darkness, dropping the lantern, then moving south and being eaten by a grue. Next time, check light with 'look' whenever the text says dark, avoid the Troll room, and after two repeats try an untried verb or exit.

LESSONS LEARNED
================================
POSITIVES TO REPEAT
1. From Behind House, open the window and go west; 'west' into the Kitchen recorded +10. Repeat this opening without detours.
2. In the Living Room, push the rug, open the trap door, then go 'down'; +25 was recorded on 'down'. Do this early.
3. Take visible valuables in new rooms, such as the Gallery art (+4 recorded this run), and keep them in inventory.
4. Before leaving a lit room, 'look' and check inventory to confirm the lantern is carried and actually lighting the room.
5. Verify each recorded score event against the action that produced it, and repeat only actions with a confirmed positive delta.

NEGATIVES TO AVOID
1. Never move or drop the lantern in a pitch-black room. This run, dropping it in the dark Troll room and then moving 'south' led to a grue death (-10).
2. Do not enter the Troll room again; the Cellar's north exit led there and an axe attack was recorded.
3. Ignore the stale Maze 'take coins' goal; it was re-issued about 20 times and drew the Actor into loops with no score.
4. Do not cycle Cellar, East Chasm, Gallery and Studio; about 350 steps earned zero. After two repeats, change verb or leave the area.
5. Stop toggling sack and lantern (put/take/open/close) and picking up junk like garlic and lunch; none of it scored and it wasted dozens of steps.
